Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the rings space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 764 and the convergence rate is O(621/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to rings theory, we decompose the problem into 764 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 621. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
